# NHANES 2017-2018 — column & answer reference
# Source: CDC codebooks (labels/codes kept close to original).
# Layout:
#   TABLE - title
#   COLUMN  short label / question
#   - code  meaning
#
# Abbreviations

_J - File suffix for the NHANES 2017-2018 cycle (each 2-year cycle has its own letter)
SS (file prefix) - Surplus specimen: lab tests run later on stored leftover blood or urine, usually on a random subsample
(Surplus) - Same as SS prefix: results from stored leftover specimens
XPT - SAS transport file format used by CDC to publish NHANES data
NHANES - National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey
CDC - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (United States)
NCHS - National Center for Health Statistics (part of CDC, runs NHANES)
SEQN - Respondent sequence number; unique participant ID and the join key across all files
SP - Sample person: the selected survey participant
HH - Household
HH ref person - Household reference person: the adult who owns or rents the home
MEC - Mobile Examination Center: the trailer where exams and blood draws happen
ACASI - Audio Computer-Assisted Self-Interview: participant answers sensitive questions privately on a computer
Proxy - Someone else answered for the participant (for example a parent for a child)
PSU - Primary sampling unit: the first-stage sampling area (usually a county)
pseudo-PSU / pseudo-stratum - Masked versions of PSU and stratum released for variance estimation
Sample weight - Number of people in the US population that a participant represents; needed for national estimates
Subsample weight - Sample weight for a random subsample (used for tests not done on everyone)
Comment code - Lab flag column: tells whether the result is at or above the detection limit or below it
Invalidity code - Scan quality flag in DXA files: tells whether a body region measurement is valid
Status code - Tells whether a component was completed, partly completed or not done
NH - Non-Hispanic
NHW - Non-Hispanic White
NHB - Non-Hispanic Black
US - United States
USDA - United States Department of Agriculture
NIH - National Institutes of Health
FTC - Federal Trade Commission (published standardized cigarette tar, nicotine and carbon monoxide contents)
WIC - Special Supplemental Nutrition Program for Women, Infants, and Children
FS - Food stamps (now called SNAP, Supplemental Nutrition Assistance Program)
CHIP - Children's Health Insurance Program
Medi-Gap - Medigap: private insurance that supplements Medicare
RR - Railroad Retirement (federal pension program for railroad workers)
UPC - Universal Product Code (barcode number)
ICD-10-CM - International Classification of Diseases, 10th Revision, Clinical Modification (diagnosis code system)
BMI - Body mass index: weight (kg) divided by height (m) squared
BP - Blood pressure
BPXO - Blood pressure measured by an automatic oscillometric device
SBP - Systolic blood pressure (the upper number)
DBP - Diastolic blood pressure (the lower number)
HBP - High blood pressure (hypertension)
HR - Heart rate
MIL - Maximum inflation level of the blood pressure cuff
DXA / DXX - Dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry: low-dose X-ray scan of bone and body composition
BMC - Bone mineral content (grams)
BMD - Bone mineral density (grams per square centimeter)
VAT - Visceral adipose tissue: fat around the organs inside the abdomen
SAT - Subcutaneous adipose tissue: fat under the skin
Android / Gynoid - Android = abdominal region; gynoid = hip and thigh region (DXA fat distribution)
L1, L2, L3, L4 - First to fourth lumbar vertebrae (lower spine)
K, D0 (written DO) - Calibration constants calculated by the DXA scanner software; D0 is written 'DO' in CDC labels
CAP - Controlled attenuation parameter: liver fat measure from elastography (dB/m)
E - In liver elastography: liver stiffness (elasticity) in kilopascals
IQRc / IQRe - Interquartile range of CAP / of stiffness E (measurement variability)
Tymp - Tympanometry: test of eardrum and middle-ear function
A1C - Hemoglobin A1C (glycohemoglobin): average blood sugar over about 3 months
HDL - High-density lipoprotein ('good' cholesterol)
LDL - Low-density lipoprotein ('bad' cholesterol)
Friedewald / Martin-Hopkins / NIH equation 2 - Three formulas for calculating LDL cholesterol
RBC - Red blood cell(s)
WBC - White blood cell(s)
Hgb / Hb - Hemoglobin
HS - High-sensitivity (as in high-sensitivity C-reactive protein)
ALT - Alanine aminotransferase (liver enzyme)
AST - Aspartate aminotransferase (liver enzyme)
ALP - Alkaline phosphatase (liver and bone enzyme)
GGT - Gamma-glutamyl transferase (liver enzyme)
LDH - Lactate dehydrogenase (enzyme released by damaged tissue)
CPK - Creatine phosphokinase (muscle enzyme)
TIBC - Total iron binding capacity
UIBC - Unsaturated iron binding capacity
AGP - Alpha-1-acid glycoprotein (inflammation marker)
SHBG - Sex hormone-binding globulin
FSH - Follicle-stimulating hormone
TST - Sex steroid hormone (testosterone) panel; used in the SSTST_J weight name
17a - 17-alpha (as in 17-alpha-hydroxyprogesterone)
PHQ-9 - Patient Health Questionnaire, 9 items (depression screener)
COPD - Chronic obstructive pulmonary disease
PID - Pelvic inflammatory disease
HPV - Human papillomavirus
HIV - Human immunodeficiency virus (HIV-1 and HIV-2 are its two types)
CMV - Cytomegalovirus
HCV - Hepatitis C virus
HDV / anti-HDV - Hepatitis D virus / antibody against it
HEV / anti-HEV - Hepatitis E virus / antibody against it
RNA - Ribonucleic acid (here: viral genetic material, shows active infection)
IgG - Immunoglobulin G: antibody showing past infection or immunity
IgM - Immunoglobulin M: antibody showing recent infection
INNO-LIA - Brand name of a line immunoassay used to confirm hepatitis C antibodies
Rx / RX - Prescription
non-RX - Non-prescription (over the counter)
Dr / DR - Doctor
ASA - Acetylsalicylic acid (aspirin)
SFA - Saturated fatty acid
MFA - Monounsaturated fatty acid
PFA - Polyunsaturated fatty acid
16:1 (and similar) - Fatty acid notation: number of carbon atoms : number of double bonds
RAE - Retinol activity equivalents (unit for vitamin A)
DFE - Dietary folate equivalents (unit for folate)
D2 / D3 - Vitamin D2 (ergocalciferol, from plants) / vitamin D3 (cholecalciferol, from skin and animal foods)
25OHD2 / 25OHD3 - 25-hydroxyvitamin D2 / D3: the blood forms of vitamin D used to judge vitamin D status
epi-25OHD3 - 3-epi-25-hydroxyvitamin D3 (a variant form of 25-hydroxyvitamin D3)
Mefox - Oxidation product of 5-methyl-tetrahydrofolate (a folate breakdown form)
DR1 / DR2 - Dietary recall day 1 / day 2
IFF / TOT - Individual foods file / total nutrient intakes file
DS1 / DS2 / DSQ - Dietary supplements day 1 / day 2 / past 30 days
IDS - Individual dietary supplements file
PFAS - Per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances ('forever chemicals')
PFOS - Perfluorooctane sulfonic acid
PFOA - Perfluorooctanoic acid
PFOSA - Perfluorooctane sulfonamide
Sm-PFOS - Sum of perfluoromethylheptane sulfonic acid isomers
Sb-PFOA - Sum of branched perfluorooctanoic acid isomers
n- (as in n-perfluorooctanoic) - Normal: the straight-chain (linear) form of a molecule
Br. / iso - Branched / isomers
GenX - Trade name for hexafluoropropylene oxide dimer acid, a PFAS replacement chemical
VOC - Volatile organic compound
MTBE - Methyl tert-butyl ether (gasoline additive)
PAH - Polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (from smoke, burnt food, exhaust)
DEET - N,N-diethyl-meta-toluamide (insect repellent)
GLYP - Glyphosate (herbicide)
TNE - Total nicotine equivalents: sum of nicotine and its metabolites (TNE-2, -3, -6, -7 differ in how many metabolites are summed)
CYHA - N-acetyl-S-(1-cyano-2-hydroxyethyl)-L-cysteine (acrylonitrile metabolite)
IPM3 - N-acetyl-S-(4-hydroxy-2-methyl-2-buten-1-yl)-L-cysteine (isoprene metabolite)
PPMA - N-acetyl-S-(6-hydroxy-2,4-cyclohexadien-1-yl)-L-cysteine (benzene metabolite)
MECP - Mono-2-ethyl-5-carboxypentyl phthalate
ECPT - Mono-2-ethyl-5-carboxypentyl terephthalate
MEHP (in this file) - Label used by CDC for mono-(2-ethyl-5-hydroxyhexyl) phthalate
MEOH - Mono-(2-ethyl-5-oxohexyl) phthalate
MHBP - Mono-3-hydroxy-n-butyl phthalate
MHHT - Mono-2-ethyl-5-hydroxyhexyl terephthalate
MHIBP - Mono-2-hydroxy-iso-butyl phthalate
MCOCH - Cyclohexane-1,2-dicarboxylic acid mono(carboxyoctyl) ester
MHNCH - Cyclohexane-1,2-dicarboxylic acid monohydroxy isononyl ester
N-acetyl-S- (also N-A-S-, N-ac-S-, N-ace-S-) - N-acetyl-S-: prefix of mercapturic acids, urine metabolites of volatile organic compounds
L-cys / L-cyst - L-cysteine
aaa- - alpha,alpha,alpha- (three substituents on the same carbon)
cis / trans - Two geometric forms of a molecule
tert - Tertiary (a branched carbon arrangement)
para - Position 4 on a benzene ring
Pb, Cd, Hg, Se, Mn, As, Cr, Co, Ni, Sn - Lead, cadmium, mercury, selenium, manganese, arsenic, chromium, cobalt, nickel, tin
USN - Urinary tin (Sn = tin)
C4H802 - CDC typo for C4H8O2 = butanoic acid
% - Percent
kg - Kilogram
g / gm - Gram
mg - Milligram (one thousandth of a gram)
mcg / ug / µg - Microgram (one millionth of a gram)
ng - Nanogram (one billionth of a gram)
pg - Picogram (one trillionth of a gram)
mol, mmol, umol, nmol, pmol - Mole (amount of substance) and its milli-, micro-, nano-, pico- fractions
L, dL, mL, uL, fL - Liter, deciliter (0.1 L), milliliter, microliter, femtoliter (volume of one blood cell)
cc - Cubic centimeter (= 1 mL)
Kg (in mmol/Kg) - Kilogram of water (osmolality unit)
/g Hb - Per gram of hemoglobin
cm, cm^2, mm - Centimeter, square centimeter, millimeter
kg/m**2 - Kilograms per square meter (BMI unit)
mm Hg - Millimeters of mercury (blood pressure unit)
IU, U, mIU, uU - International unit, enzyme unit, milli-international unit, micro-unit (enzyme and hormone activity)
kcal - Kilocalorie (food calorie)
kPa - Kilopascal (pressure / stiffness unit)
daPa - Decapascal (ear pressure unit, 10 pascals)
dB, db - Decibel (sound level; hearing threshold)
dB/m - Decibels per meter (CAP liver fat unit)
Hz - Hertz (sound frequency, cycles per second)
lbs - Pounds (weight)
SI (variable name suffix) - International System of Units version of a lab value
# - Number of
@ - At
+ (in 10+) - Or more
w/ - With
b/w - Between
b/c - Because
Avg - Average
Cmt / Comt / cmt cd / cd - Comment code
Conc - Concentration
excl / incl - Excluding / including
info - Information
meds - Medications
mo / mos - Month / months
yr / yrs - Year / years
hrs / Hrs - Hours
wk - Week
sec - Seconds
7-d / 30 d - 7 days / 30 days
12m - 12 months
HH:MM - Clock time as hours:minutes
9am - 5pm - Between 9 in the morning and 5 in the afternoon
wt - Weight
rdg - Reading
pres - Pressure
resp. - Respondent
ref - Reference
prof - Professional
preg. - Pregnancy
num - Number
oc - Occasion
diff - Difficulty
suppl. - Supplements
Tot - Total
Rt - Right
CH - Child (in food security questions)
WM - Woman (in food security questions)
Cig / cigs - Cigarette / cigarettes
ID - Identifier
TV - Television
Ed - Education
1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th - First, second, third, fourth
MCQ230a (and similar) - Reference to another variable in the same file
prescriptn, refrig, fracd., overnite, tonge, standingup - CDC spelling shortcuts: prescription, refrigerated, fractured, overnight, tongue, standing up
{you/SP}, {he/she}, {DISPLAY ...} - Fill-in text: the interviewer reads the version that fits the respondent
CAPI - Computer-Assisted Personal Interview (interviewer reads questions from a laptop)
SNAP - Supplemental Nutrition Assistance Program (formerly food stamps)
EBT - Electronic Benefit Transfer card (how SNAP benefits are paid)
SSI - Supplemental Security Income
HHS - US Department of Health and Human Services
HMO - Health Maintenance Organization (type of health insurance plan)
VA / Champ-VA / Tricare - Veterans Affairs / its family health program / military health insurance
GED - General Educational Development test (high school equivalency)
AA degree - Associate of Arts degree (2-year college degree)
K-12 - Kindergarten through 12th grade (primary and secondary school)
DC - District of Columbia (Washington, DC)
AIDS - Acquired immunodeficiency syndrome
HBV - Hepatitis B virus
hs-CRP / CRP - (High-sensitivity) C-reactive protein
PFNA / PFUA / PFDE / PFHxS - Perfluorononanoic / perfluoroundecanoic / perfluorodecanoic acid / perfluorohexane sulfonic acid
MEA - 3-(Ethylcarbamoyl) benzoic acid (DEET metabolite)
MSG - Monosodium glutamate
Vit - Vitamin
TM - Tympanic membrane (eardrum)
FM system - Wireless hearing assistance device
LI / CI (teeth) - Lateral incisor / central incisor
IQR / M - Interquartile range / median
M / XL (probe) - Medium / extra large ultrasound probe used for liver elastography
XX / XXX - Placeholder digits in CDC notes about how a number is shown
AM / PM - Morning / afternoon-evening
DS - Nintendo DS (handheld game console)
GPC, NXT, MCD - Cigarette brand or sub-brand names, not abbreviations to decode
Range of Values - Continuous measurement: the column holds a number in this range, not a code
Missing / . - No value recorded
Refused / Don't know - Participant refused to answer / did not know (usually codes 7, 77, 777 / 9, 99, 999)

AUAREQC  Right Ear Tympanogram Quality Code
- 1  Good - tympanogram is smooth and symmetrical
- 2  Adequate - tympanogram is 'noisy' but adequate for interpretation
- 3  Poor - tympanogram cannot be interpreted
- 7  SP Refused Tympanometry
- 8  Could not obtain
- 9  Equivocal - tympanogram cannot be evaluated without further information
- .  Missing
AUATYMTR  Tympanogram Type, Right Ear
- Type A  Normal
- Type AS  Normal, but the ear drum is unusually stiff
- Type AD  Normal, but the ear drum is unusually flaccid
- Type B  Flat, no measurable ear drum mobility
- Type C  Normal ear drum mobility, but negative middle ear pressure
- 88888  Could not obtain
- 99999  Could not interpret
- < blank >  Missing

AUATYMTL  Tympanogram Type, Left Ear
- Type A  Normal
- Type AS  Normal, but the ear drum is unusually stiff
- Type AD  Normal, but the ear drum is unusually flaccid
- Type B  Flat, no measurable ear drum mobility
- Type C  Normal ear drum mobility, but negative middle ear pressure
- 88888  Could not obtain
- 99999  Could not interpret
- < blank >  Missing
